<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#102d45">
  <meta name="description" content="AlignedCardio digital marketing performance dashboard for participating cardiology practices.">
  <title>AlignedCardio | SEO &amp; Inbound Marketing Performance</title>

  <link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%2064%2064'%3E%3Crect%20width='64'%20height='64'%20rx='14'%20fill='%23102d45'/%3E%3Cpath%20d='M8%2034h12l6-12%2010%2024%207-12h13'%20fill='none'%20stroke='%2377d2c9'%20stroke-width='5'%20stroke-linecap='round'%20stroke-linejoin='round'/%3E%3C/svg%3E">
  <style>
/* Variables */
:root {
  --navy: #102d45;
  --navy2: #173f5b;
  --teal: #087e82;
  --mint: #dff4ef;
  --blue: #3266a8;
  --bg: #f5f8fa;
  --panel: #fff;
  --ink: #183247;
  --muted: #64778a;
  --line: #e0e9ef;
  --good: #07866d;
  --bad: #bd5c50;
  --amber: #a56923;
  --shadow: 0 12px 36px rgba(18, 47, 70, 0.045);
  --radius: 16px;
  --sidebar: 242px;

  font-family: Inter, ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, Segoe UI, sans-serif;
  color-scheme: light;
}

body.dark {
  --navy: #0c1c2b;
  --navy2: #16364c;
  --teal: #58c9c1;
  --mint: #193e3e;
  --blue: #7bb4f0;
  --bg: #0d1925;
  --panel: #172838;
  --ink: #e8f3f7;
  --muted: #a2b6c5;
  --line: #2d4353;
  --good: #60d6b2;
  --bad: #f28b80;
  --amber: #e6b775;
  --shadow: none;

  color-scheme: dark;
}

/* Reset */
* {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

html {
  scroll-behavior: smooth;
  scroll-padding-top: 94px;
}

body {
  margin: 0;
  background: var(--bg);
  color: var(--ink);
  font-size: 15px;
  line-height: 1.5;
}

button,
input,
select {
  font: inherit;
}

button {
  cursor: pointer;
}

a {
  color: inherit;
  text-decoration: none;
}

h1,
h2,
h3,
h4,
p {
  margin: 0;
}

h1 {
  font-size: clamp(1.7rem, 2.5vw, 2.35rem);
  line-height: 1.2;
  letter-spacing: -0.035em;
}

h2 {
  font-size: 1.43rem;
  letter-spacing: -0.025em;
}

h3 {
  font-size: 1rem;
}

h4 {
  font-size: 0.9rem;
}

small {
  font-size: 0.76rem;
}

input,
select,
button {
  min-height: 40px;
}

input,
select {
  background: var(--panel);
  color: var(--ink);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 9px;
  padding: 9px 12px;
  max-width: 100%;
}

:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid #5ccfc3;
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

[hidden] {
  display: none !important;
}

.skip {
  position: absolute;
  left: -9999px;
  z-index: 100;
}

.skip:focus {
  left: 12px;
  top: 12px;
  background: #fff;
  padding: 10px;
}

/* Layout */
.app {
  display: flex;
  min-height: 100vh;
}

.sidebar {
  width: var(--sidebar);
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0 auto 0 0;
  background: var(--navy);
  color: #d9e7ef;
  padding: 26px 16px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  z-index: 30;
}

.main-wrap {
  margin-left: var(--sidebar);
  width: calc(100% - var(--sidebar));
  min-width: 0;
}

main {
  padding: 32px clamp(20px, 3vw, 48px) 24px;
  max-width: 1700px;
  margin: auto;
}

.dashboard-section {
  margin-top: 54px;
  scroll-margin-top: 100px;
}

.section-heading {
  display: flex;
  align-items: end;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 20px;
  margin: 0 0 17px;
}

.section-heading .muted,
.source-label {
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.82rem;
}

.eyebrow {
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  font-weight: 800;
  color: var(--teal);
}

.section-heading h2 {
  margin-top: 3px;
}

.page-intro {
  display: flex;
  align-items: end;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 22px;
  margin-bottom: 25px;
}

.page-intro h1 {
  margin-top: 10px;
}

.page-intro p {
  color: var(--muted);
  margin-top: 8px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.demo-tag {
  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
  background: var(--mint);
  color: var(--teal);
  border-radius: 4px;
  padding: 4px 6px;
  margin-left: 8px;
}

.filters {
  display: flex;
  gap: 10px;
}

.filters label,
.custom-range label {
  display: grid;
  gap: 5px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.7rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  font-weight: 750;
}

.filters select {
  min-width: 205px;
  letter-spacing: 0;
  font-size: 0.87rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.custom-range {
  display: flex;
  align-items: end;
  gap: 10px;
  margin: -10px 0 20px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.custom-range #dateError {
  color: var(--bad);
  font-size: 0.86rem;
}

/* Header */
.topbar {
  height: 76px;
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  z-index: 20;
  background: var(--panel);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 0 clamp(20px, 3vw, 48px);
  gap: 12px;
}

.header-title {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  line-height: 1.35;
}

.header-title strong {
  font-size: 0.94rem;
}

.header-title span {
  font-size: 0.77rem;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.header-actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
}

.week-pill {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 40px;
  padding: 8px 13px;
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--teal);
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.button {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 9px;
  padding: 9px 15px;
  font-size: 0.85rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  background: var(--panel);
  color: var(--ink);
}

.button:hover,
.icon-button:hover {
  border-color: var(--teal);
  color: var(--teal);
}

.button.primary {
  background: var(--navy2);
  border-color: var(--navy2);
  color: #fff;
}

.icon-button {
  width: 40px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 9px;
  background: var(--panel);
  color: var(--ink);
  font-size: 1.1rem;
}

.menu {
  display: none;
}

/* Sidebar */
.brand {
  display: flex;
  gap: 11px;
  align-items: center;
  color: white;
  font-size: 1.15rem;
  line-height: 1.05;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.03em;
  margin: 4px 7px 37px;
}

.brand-accent {
  color: #8fe5dc;
}

.brand small {
  display: block;
  color: #9ab7c7;
  font-size: 0.55rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.13em;
  margin-top: 6px;
}

.mark {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 36px;
  height: 36px;
  border-radius: 9px;
  background: #27637a;
  color: #a6f1e4;
  font-size: 2rem;
  font-weight: 400;
}

.sidebar nav {
  display: grid;
  gap: 5px;
}

.sidebar nav a {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  padding: 10px 13px;
  border-radius: 9px;
  font-size: 0.84rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  min-height: 42px;
}

.sidebar nav a:hover,
.sidebar nav a.active {
  background: #224c62;
  color: #fff;
}

.sidebar nav a:first-letter {
  color: #96d8d8;
}

.side-bottom {
  margin: auto 9px 7px;
  border-top: 1px solid #34566b;
  padding-top: 18px;
  color: #b4cbd4;
  font-size: 0.69rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.side-bottom small {
  letter-spacing: 0;
  color: #8cabb9;
  font-weight: 400;
}

.demo-dot {
  display: inline-block;
  width: 7px;
  height: 7px;
  background: #70d5bb;
  border-radius: 50%;
  margin-right: 5px;
}

/* KPI Cards */
.kpi-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 14px;
}

.kpi,
.mini,
.panel,
.tech-card,
.insight-card {
  background: var(--panel);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow);
}

.kpi {
  padding: 21px 23px;
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
  transition: transform 0.2s, box-shadow 0.2s;
}

.kpi:hover,
.panel:hover {
  box-shadow: 0 15px 40px rgba(18, 47, 70, 0.08);
}

.kpi:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.kpi::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  left: 0;
  top: 24px;
  bottom: 24px;
  width: 3px;
  background: var(--teal);
  border-radius: 2px;
}

.metric-label {
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.84rem;
  font-weight: 650;
}

.metric-main {
  display: flex;
  align-items: baseline;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 5px;
  margin: 8px 0 4px;
}

.metric-value {
  font-size: clamp(1.6rem, 2.3vw, 2.15rem);
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.04em;
  line-height: 1.15;
}

.spark {
  width: 74px;
  height: 30px;
  flex: none;
}

.comparison {
  font-size: 0.76rem;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.delta {
  font-weight: 800;
}

.delta.good {
  color: var(--good);
}

.delta.bad {
  color: var(--bad);
}

.delta.neutral {
  color: var(--muted);
}

.insight-strip {
  margin-top: 15px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-left: 3px solid var(--teal);
  background: var(--panel);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 12px 17px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.88rem;
}

.insight-strip strong {
  color: var(--ink);
}

.mini-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 15px;
}

.mini-grid.five {
  grid-template-columns: repeat(5, minmax(0, 1fr));
}

.mini {
  padding: 18px;
}

.mini strong {
  display: block;
  font-size: 1.55rem;
  letter-spacing: -0.035em;
  margin: 5px 0 2px;
}

.mini .metric-label {
  font-size: 0.76rem;
}

.mini small {
  color: var(--muted);
}

/* Charts */
.chart-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 15px;
}

.chart-grid.two {
  margin-bottom: 15px;
}

.panel {
  padding: 20px 22px;
  min-width: 0;
  margin-bottom: 15px;
}

.chart-grid .panel {
  margin-bottom: 0;
}

.chart-grid .wide {
  grid-column: span 2;
}

.panel-head {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 15px;
}

.panel-head p {
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.81rem;
  margin-top: 2px;
}

.chart-box {
  height: 270px;
  position: relative;
}

.chart-box.short {
  height: 210px;
}

.chart-box canvas {
  max-width: 100%;
  max-height: 100%;
}

.source-label {
  background: var(--mint);
  padding: 5px 10px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  color: var(--teal);
  font-weight: 700;
}

/* Tables */
.table-panel {
  padding-bottom: 4px;
}

.table-scroll {
  overflow: auto;
  max-width: 100%;
}

table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  text-align: left;
  white-space: nowrap;
  font-size: 0.82rem;
}

th {
  background: var(--bg);
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.7rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.045em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  font-weight: 800;
}

td,
th {
  padding: 11px 12px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
}

tbody tr:hover {
  background: var(--bg);
}

tbody tr:last-child td {
  border-bottom: 0;
}

td:first-child {
  font-weight: 650;
}

td a {
  color: var(--blue);
}

.table-tools {
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.table-tools input {
  min-width: 190px;
}

.table-tools select {
  max-width: 150px;
}

.panel-head.wrap {
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.table-panel .panel-head {
  padding-bottom: 5px;
}

.empty {
  color: var(--muted);
  text-align: center;
  padding: 25px;
}

/* Badges */
.badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  border-radius: 5px;
  padding: 3px 7px;
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  background: var(--bg);
  color: var(--muted);
}

.badge.improved,
.badge.healthy,
.badge.converted {
  background: rgba(20, 164, 125, 0.12);
  color: var(--good);
}

.badge.declined,
.badge.critical {
  background: rgba(205, 91, 76, 0.12);
  color: var(--bad);
}

.badge.new,
.badge.attention {
  background: rgba(212, 150, 58, 0.14);
  color: var(--amber);
}

.badge.stable {
  background: rgba(84, 134, 178, 0.14);
  color: var(--blue);
}

.funnel {
  display: grid;
  gap: 12px;
}

.funnel-row {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 165px 1fr 50px;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  font-size: 0.81rem;
}

.track {
  height: 13px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  background: var(--bg);
  overflow: hidden;
}

.fill {
  height: 100%;
  border-radius: 20px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--teal), #81cabe);
}

.funnel-row strong {
  text-align: right;
}

.summary-card h3 {
  margin-bottom: 18px;
}

.summary-columns {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 20px;
}

.summary-columns h4 {
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}

.summary-columns ul {
  margin: 0;
  padding-left: 18px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.summary-columns li {
  margin-bottom: 9px;
  font-size: 0.84rem;
}

.tech-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 15px;
}

.tech-card {
  padding: 15px 17px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 12px;
}

.tech-card strong {
  font-size: 1.2rem;
}

.tech-card .metric-label {
  display: block;
  font-size: 0.76rem;
}

.detail-note {
  background: var(--mint);
  padding: 14px;
  border-radius: 9px;
  margin-bottom: 15px;
  color: var(--ink);
}

.insights-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 15px;
}

.insight-card {
  padding: 18px 19px;
}

.insight-card span {
  color: var(--teal);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.insight-card p {
  margin-top: 7px;
  font-weight: 650;
  font-size: 0.88rem;
}

footer {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 15px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--line);
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  margin-top: 48px;
  padding-top: 22px;
}

.export-menu {
  position: fixed;
  right: 28px;
  top: 64px;
  z-index: 50;
  background: var(--panel);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 11px;
  box-shadow: 0 18px 40px #0002;
  padding: 7px;
  min-width: 178px;
}

.export-menu button {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  text-align: left;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--ink);
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 9px;
}

.export-menu button:hover {
  background: var(--bg);
}

.toast {
  position: fixed;
  right: 20px;
  bottom: 20px;
  background: var(--navy);
  color: #fff;
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 12px 16px;
  z-index: 60;
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  transition: opacity 0.2s;
}

.toast.show {
  opacity: 1;
}

/* Responsive Design */
@media (max-width: 1200px) {
  .kpi-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  }

  .mini-grid.five {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  }

  .filters {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }

  .page-intro {
    align-items: start;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 950px) {
  .sidebar {
    transform: translateX(-100%);
    transition: transform 0.25s;
  }

  .sidebar.open {
    transform: none;
  }

  .main-wrap {
    margin-left: 0;
    width: 100%;
  }

  .menu {
    display: block;
  }

  .mobile-scrim:not([hidden]) {
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    background: #06182491;
    z-index: 25;
  }

  .page-intro {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }

  .filters {
    width: 100%;
  }

  .filters label {
    flex: 1;
  }

  .filters select {
    width: 100%;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 700px) {
  main {
    padding: 23px 15px;
  }

  .topbar {
    height: 66px;
    padding: 0 14px;
  }

  .header-title strong {
    font-size: 0.79rem;
    max-width: 190px;
  }

  .week-pill {
    display: none;
  }

  .export-button {
    font-size: 0.73rem;
    padding: 7px;
    min-height: 36px;
  }

  .kpi-grid,
  .chart-grid,
  .chart-grid.two,
  .mini-grid,
  .mini-grid.five,
  .tech-grid,
  .insights-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  }

  .chart-grid .wide {
    grid-column: span 2;
  }

  .filters {
    flex-direction: column;
  }

  .section-heading {
    align-items: start;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }

  .panel {
    padding: 17px;
  }

  .summary-columns {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .chart-box {
    height: 240px;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 480px) {
  .kpi-grid,
  .chart-grid,
  .chart-grid.two,
  .mini-grid,
  .mini-grid.five,
  .tech-grid,
  .insights-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .chart-grid .wide {
    grid-column: span 1;
  }

  .kpi {
    padding: 17px 21px;
  }

  .funnel-row {
    grid-template-columns: 110px 1fr 32px;
  }

  .header-title span {
    font-size: 0.66rem;
  }

  footer {
    flex-direction: column;
  }
}

/* Dark Mode */
body.dark .sidebar {
  background: #071522;
}

body.dark .sidebar nav a.active,
body.dark .sidebar nav a:hover {
  background: #1a4052;
}

body.dark .button.primary {
  background: #237783;
  border-color: #237783;
}

body.dark .toast {
  background: #245263;
}

/* Print Styles */
@media print {
  @page {
    size: A4;
    margin: 12mm;
  }

  body {
    background: #fff !important;
    color: #172d3d !important;
    font-size: 10pt;
  }

  .sidebar,
  .topbar,
  .filters,
  .custom-range,
  .table-tools,
  .button,
  .icon-button,
  .export-menu,
  .toast,
  .mobile-scrim {
    display: none !important;
  }

  .main-wrap {
    width: 100%;
    margin: 0;
  }

  main {
    padding: 0;
    max-width: none;
  }

  .page-intro {
    margin-bottom: 12px;
  }

  .dashboard-section {
    margin-top: 25px;
    break-before: auto;
  }

  .panel,
  .kpi,
  .mini,
  .tech-card,
  .insight-card {
    box-shadow: none;
    break-inside: avoid;
    border: 1px solid #ddd;
  }

  .chart-grid {
    display: block;
  }

  .chart-grid .panel {
    margin-bottom: 12px;
  }

  .chart-box {
    height: 190px;
  }

  .table-scroll {
    overflow: visible;
  }

  table {
    white-space: normal;
    font-size: 8pt;
  }

  td,
  th {
    padding: 5px;
  }

  .section-heading {
    margin-bottom: 8px;
  }

  .kpi-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  }

  .mini-grid,
  .mini-grid.five {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
  }

  .source-label,
  .demo-tag {
    color: #167e82 !important;
  }

  footer {
    margin-top: 15px;
  }
}
/* JRC data update: retain the dashboard system and clarify source coverage. */
.brand{font-size:1.02rem;line-height:1.2}
.brand .mark{font-size:.83rem;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:-.04em;flex:none}
.brand small{line-height:1.3}
.side-bottom{font-size:.68rem}
.sidebar nav a{font-size:.82rem}
.sidebar nav{gap:2px}
.kpi-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr))}
.kpi{min-height:145px}
.kpi .metric-value{font-size:clamp(1.5rem,2vw,2rem)}
.metric-source{display:block;margin-top:10px;color:var(--muted);font-size:.7rem;line-height:1.35}
.metric-help{font-size:.82rem;cursor:help;color:var(--teal);margin-left:3px}
.mini-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr))}
.mini-grid.five{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr))}
.chart-grid.two{margin-bottom:15px}
.table-note{padding:6px 12px 14px;color:var(--muted);font-size:.78rem}
.unavailable{height:100%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;text-align:center;padding:18px;color:var(--muted);background:var(--bg);border:1px dashed var(--line);border-radius:10px;font-size:.9rem}
.summary-card .muted{color:var(--muted);font-size:.91rem;line-height:1.65}
#querySearch{min-width:200px}
#pageSort,#monthlySort{max-width:190px}
.kpi .comparison{min-height:19px}
body.dark .metric-help{color:var(--teal)}
@media(max-width:1300px){.kpi-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr))}}
@media(max-width:1050px){.kpi-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}}
@media(max-width:700px){.kpi-grid,.mini-grid,.mini-grid.five{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}.kpi{min-height:136px}.source-label{max-width:100%;white-space:normal}.header-title strong{max-width:200px}}
@media(max-width:480px){.kpi-grid,.mini-grid,.mini-grid.five{grid-template-columns:1fr}.kpi{min-height:0}.header-title strong{font-size:.72rem;max-width:160px}.export-button{font-size:.68rem}}
@media print{.kpi-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr)}.mini-grid,.mini-grid.five{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr)}.unavailable{min-height:80px}.kpi{min-height:0}.metric-source{color:#555}}
/* Shared brand stays fixed while the selected practice changes. */
.brand .mark{font-size:2rem;font-weight:400;letter-spacing:0}
.filters{align-items:end;flex-wrap:wrap}
.filters label{min-width:185px}
@media(max-width:700px){.filters label{min-width:0}}

  </style>

</head>

<body>
  <a class="skip" href="#overview">Skip to dashboard</a>

  <div class="app">
    <aside class="sidebar" id="sidebar">
      <div class="brand">
<span class="mark" aria-hidden="true">⌁</span><span>Aligned<span class="brand-accent">Cardio</span><small>MARKETING PERFORMANCE</small></span>
</div>
<nav aria-label="Dashboard sections"><a href="#overview" class="active">◫ <span>Overview</span></a><a href="#analytics">◉ <span>GA4 Analytics</span></a><a href="#search-console">⌕ <span>Search Console</span></a><a href="#rankings">▥ <span>Keyword Rankings</span></a><a href="#leads">♧ <span>Organic Leads</span></a><a href="#pages">▤ <span>Landing Pages</span></a><a href="#monthly">▦ <span>Monthly Performance</span></a><a href="#insights">◇ <span>SEO Insights</span></a><a href="#action-items">☑ <span>Action Items</span></a></nav><div class="side-bottom">
<span class="demo-dot"></span> SOURCE DATA<br><small id="sourceCoverage">5 of 6 practices available</small>
</div></aside>

    <div class="main-wrap">
      <header class="topbar">
        <button class="icon-button menu" id="menuButton" aria-label="Open navigation" aria-expanded="false">☰</button>

        <div class="header-title">
          <strong>AlignedCardio · SEO Performance</strong>
          <span id="currentDate"></span>
        </div>

        <div class="header-actions">
          <span class="week-pill" id="weekLabel">Latest available</span>
          <button class="icon-button" id="themeButton" aria-label="Switch to dark mode" title="Toggle theme">◐</button>
          <button class="button secondary export-button" id="printHeader">
            Export Report ↗
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main id="overview">
<div class="page-intro">
<div>
<div class="eyebrow">ALIGNEDCARDIO <span class="demo-tag">SOURCE DATA</span>
</div>
<h1>Digital marketing performance</h1>
<p id="periodLabel">Reporting windows: —</p>
</div>
<div class="filters">
<label>SELECT PRACTICE<select id="practiceSelect"><option value="all">All Practices</option>
<option value="james">James River Cardiology</option>
<option value="colonial">Colonial Heart</option>
<option value="hra">Heart Rhythm Associates</option>
<option value="nova">Nova Cardiocare</option>
<option value="swva">Southwest Virginia Cardiology</option>
<option value="rethink">Rethink AFib</option></select></label><label>REPORT VIEW<select id="rangeSelect"><option value="current">Current Period</option>
<option value="previous">Previous Period</option>
<option value="monthly">Monthly View</option></select></label>
</div>
</div>
<div class="section-heading">
<div>
<span class="eyebrow">EXECUTIVE OVERVIEW</span><h2 id="overviewTitle">Latest available data</h2>
</div>
<span class="muted" id="comparisonLabel"></span>
</div>
<div class="kpi-grid" id="executiveKpis"></div>
<div class="insight-strip" id="quickInsight"></div>
<section id="analytics" class="dashboard-section"><div class="section-heading">
<div>
<span class="eyebrow">01 / AUDIENCE</span><h2>Google Analytics 4 Performance</h2>
</div>
<span class="source-label" id="gaPeriod"></span>
</div>
<div class="mini-grid" id="gaMetrics"></div>
<div class="chart-grid">
<article class="panel wide"><div class="panel-head"><div>
<h3>Organic Traffic Trend</h3>
<p>Daily organic sessions</p>
</div></div>
<div class="chart-box" id="trafficBox"><canvas id="trafficChart" aria-label="Organic traffic trend" role="img"></canvas></div></article><article class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div>
<h3>Sessions by Channel</h3>
<p>Session primary channel group</p>
</div></div>
<div class="chart-box"><canvas id="sourceChart" aria-label="Sessions by channel" role="img"></canvas></div></article><article class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div>
<h3>Users vs Sessions</h3>
<p>All channels, different GA4 scopes</p>
</div></div>
<div class="chart-box"><canvas id="landingChart" aria-label="Users and sessions" role="img"></canvas></div></article><article class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div>
<h3>Engagement by Channel</h3>
<p>Engaged sessions divided by sessions</p>
</div></div>
<div class="chart-box"><canvas id="engagementChart" aria-label="Engagement by channel" role="img"></canvas></div></article><article class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div>
<h3>Organic Leads / Conversions</h3>
<p>New leads attributed to first-user Organic Search</p>
</div></div>
<div class="chart-box" id="gaLeadsBox"><canvas id="gaLeadsChart" aria-label="Organic new leads" role="img"></canvas></div></article>
</div></section>
<section id="search-console" class="dashboard-section"><div class="section-heading">
<div>
<span class="eyebrow">02 / SEARCH VISIBILITY</span><h2>Google Search Console Performance</h2>
</div>
<span class="source-label" id="gscPeriod"></span>
</div>
<div class="mini-grid" id="gscMetrics"></div>
<div class="chart-grid two">
<article class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div>
<h3>Clicks and Impressions</h3>
<p>Daily Web search performance; separate axes</p>
</div></div>
<div class="chart-box" id="searchBox"><canvas id="searchChart" aria-label="Search clicks and impressions" role="img"></canvas></div></article><article class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div>
<h3>Average Position</h3>
<p>Lower is better</p>
</div></div>
<div class="chart-box" id="positionBox"><canvas id="positionChart" aria-label="Average search position" role="img"></canvas></div></article><article class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div>
<h3>Search CTR</h3>
<p>Daily clicks divided by impressions</p>
</div></div>
<div class="chart-box" id="ctrBox"><canvas id="ctrChart" aria-label="Search click-through rate" role="img"></canvas></div></article>
</div>
<article class="panel table-panel"><div class="panel-head wrap">
<div>
<h3>Search Queries</h3>
<p>Query rows follow each practice’s own Search Console export window.</p>
</div>
<input id="querySearch" type="search" aria-label="Search queries" placeholder="Search queries">
</div>
<div class="table-scroll"><table>
<thead><tr>
<th>Practice</th>
<th>Keyword</th>
<th>Clicks</th>
<th>Impressions</th>
<th>CTR</th>
<th>Avg. Position</th>
<th>Ranking Change</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody id="queryRows"></tbody>
</table></div>
<p class="table-note" id="queryCount"></p></article></section>
<section id="rankings" class="dashboard-section"><div class="section-heading">
<div>
<span class="eyebrow">03 / RANKINGS</span><h2>Keyword Ranking Performance</h2>
</div>
<span class="source-label">Aug 16 → Sep 27, 2026</span>
</div>
<div class="mini-grid five" id="rankMetrics"></div>
<article class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div>
<h3>Current Rank Distribution</h3>
<p>Tracked location-level keyword rows; exclusive buckets</p>
</div></div>
<div class="chart-box short"><canvas id="distributionChart" aria-label="Keyword rank distribution" role="img"></canvas></div></article><article class="panel table-panel"><div class="panel-head wrap">
<div>
<h3>Tracked Keywords</h3>
<p>Each location is a separate tracked row</p>
</div>
<div class="table-tools">
<input id="keywordSearch" type="search" placeholder="Search keyword or URL" aria-label="Search keywords"><select id="statusFilter" aria-label="Filter ranking status"><option value="all">All statuses</option>
<option>Improved</option>
<option>Declined</option>
<option>No Change</option>
<option>No Prior Rank</option>
<option>Reported Lost</option>
<option>Unranked</option></select><select id="rankSort" aria-label="Sort keywords"><option value="rank">Current rank</option>
<option value="change">Ranking change</option>
<option value="keyword">Keyword A–Z</option>
<option value="location">Location A–Z</option></select>
</div>
</div>
<div class="table-scroll"><table>
<thead><tr>
<th>Practice</th>
<th>Keyword</th>
<th>Location</th>
<th>Current Rank</th>
<th>Previous Rank</th>
<th>Change</th>
<th>Search Volume</th>
<th>Category</th>
<th>Priority</th>
<th>URL / Landing Page</th>
<th>SEO Status</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody id="keywordRows"></tbody>
</table></div></article></section>
<section id="leads" class="dashboard-section"><div class="section-heading">
<div>
<span class="eyebrow">04 / ACQUISITION</span><h2>Organic Lead Performance</h2>
</div>
<span class="source-label">GA4: Sep 21–27, 2026</span>
</div>
<div class="mini-grid" id="leadMetrics"></div>
<div class="chart-grid two">
<article class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div>
<h3>Organic Lead Reporting</h3>
<p>GA4 organic new leads for the available week</p>
</div></div>
<div class="chart-box" id="leadsBox"><canvas id="leadsChart" aria-label="Organic new leads in reported week" role="img"></canvas></div></article><article class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div>
<h3>Self-Reported Sources</h3>
<p>Full supplied workbook · all self-reported sources</p>
</div></div>
<div class="chart-box"><canvas id="sourceLeadsChart" aria-label="Self-reported lead source rows" role="img"></canvas></div></article>
</div>
<div class="detail-note">The reported lead row total covers the full supplied workbook and includes all self-reported sources. The date columns have no headers, so it cannot be filtered reliably to the GA4 week. Online Search may include paid search. GA4 organic new leads are a separate weekly metric; no identifiers are displayed.</div>
<article class="panel table-panel"><div class="panel-head wrap"><div>
<h3>Organic Lead Detail</h3>
<p>Only the reporting period and channel are available</p>
</div></div>
<div class="table-scroll"><table>
<thead><tr>
<th>Date / Month</th>
<th>Landing Page</th>
<th>Lead Type</th>
<th>Source / Medium</th>
<th>Leads</th>
<th>Conversion Rate</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody id="dailyLeadRows"></tbody>
</table></div></article></section>
<section id="pages" class="dashboard-section"><div class="section-heading">
<div>
<span class="eyebrow">05 / CONTENT</span><h2>Landing Pages</h2>
</div>
<span class="source-label">GA4 Sep 21–27 · GSC Aug 29–Sep 25</span>
</div>
<div class="chart-grid two">
<article class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div>
<h3>Top GA4 Landing Pages</h3>
<p>Sessions from all channels</p>
</div></div>
<div class="chart-box"><canvas id="pageChart" aria-label="Top landing pages by sessions" role="img"></canvas></div></article><article class="panel summary-card"><h3>Page Data Coverage</h3>
<p class="muted">GA4 page users and sessions include all channels. Organic-only page users and organic leads by page were not supplied. Search Console page metrics follow each practice’s own reporting window.</p></article>
</div>
<article class="panel table-panel"><div class="panel-head wrap">
<div>
<h3>Page Performance</h3>
<p>Matched by URL path; dates and scopes are shown above</p>
</div>
<select id="pageSort" aria-label="Sort landing pages"><option value="sessions">GA4 sessions</option>
<option value="users">GA4 users</option>
<option value="clicks">GSC clicks</option>
<option value="impressions">GSC impressions</option>
<option value="ctr">GSC CTR</option></select>
</div>
<div class="table-scroll"><table>
<thead><tr>
<th>Practice</th>
<th>Page URL</th>
<th>Organic Users</th>
<th>GA4 Users</th>
<th>GA4 Sessions</th>
<th>Organic Leads</th>
<th>GSC Clicks</th>
<th>Impressions</th>
<th>CTR</th>
<th>Avg. Position</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody id="pageRows"></tbody>
</table></div></article></section>
<section id="monthly" class="dashboard-section"><div class="section-heading"><div>
<span class="eyebrow">06 / PERIODS</span><h2>Monthly Performance</h2>
</div></div>
<article class="panel table-panel"><div class="panel-head wrap">
<div>
<h3>Available Periods</h3>
<p>Only supplied periods are shown; N/A means no comparable export.</p>
</div>
<select id="monthlySort" aria-label="Sort monthly table"><option value="dateDesc">Newest period</option>
<option value="dateAsc">Oldest period</option>
<option value="clicks">Search clicks</option></select>
</div>
<div class="table-scroll"><table>
<thead><tr>
<th>Month / Coverage</th>
<th>Organic Users</th>
<th>Organic Sessions</th>
<th>Search Clicks</th>
<th>Impressions</th>
<th>CTR</th>
<th>Avg. Position</th>
<th>Organic Leads</th>
<th>Lead Conversion Rate</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody id="monthlyRows"></tbody>
</table></div></article></section>
<section id="insights" class="dashboard-section"><div class="section-heading"><div>
<span class="eyebrow">07 / FINDINGS</span><h2>SEO Insights</h2>
</div></div>
<div class="insights-grid" id="managementInsights"></div></section>
<section id="action-items" class="dashboard-section"><div class="section-heading"><div>
<span class="eyebrow">08 / NEXT STEPS</span><h2>SEO Action Items</h2>
</div></div>
<article class="panel table-panel"><div class="panel-head"><div>
<h3>Recommended Actions</h3>
<p>Generated from supplied rankings and search performance</p>
</div></div>
<div class="table-scroll"><table>
<thead><tr>
<th>Priority</th>
<th>Action</th>
<th>Evidence</th>
<th>Area</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody id="actionRows"></tbody>
</table></div></article></section>
<footer>AlignedCardio | Digital Marketing Performance Dashboard <span id="lastUpdated"></span></footer>
</main>
</div>
  </div>

  <div class="mobile-scrim" id="scrim" hidden></div>
  <div class="toast" id="toast" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div>

  <div class="export-menu" id="exportMenu" hidden>
    <button id="exportCsv">Export CSV</button>
    <button id="printReport">Print Report</button>
    <button id="downloadPdf">Download PDF</button>
  </div>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js@4.4.8/dist/chart.umd.min.js"></script>

const practiceNames={all:'All Practices',james:'James River Cardiology',colonial:'Colonial Heart',hra:'Heart Rhythm Associates',nova:'Nova Cardiocare',swva:'Southwest Virginia Cardiology',rethink:'Rethink AFib'};
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id),NA='N/A';
const state={practice:'all',view:'current',keywordSearch:'',querySearch:'',status:'all',rankSort:'rank',pageSort:'sessions',monthlySort:'dateDesc',charts:{}};
const fmt=n=>n==null||!Number.isFinite(n)?NA:new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US').format(Math.round(n));
const percent=n=>n==null||!Number.isFinite(n)?NA:`${(n*100).toFixed(1)}%`;
const pos=n=>n==null||!Number.isFinite(n)?NA:n.toFixed(2);
const duration=n=>n==null||!Number.isFinite(n)?NA:`${Math.floor(n/60)}m ${Math.round(n%60)}s`;
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const rate=(a,b)=>a==null||b==null||b===0?null:a/b;
const sum=(rows,key)=>rows.reduce((n,r)=>n+(r[key]||0),0);
const short=iso=>new Date(`${iso}T12:00:00`).toLocaleDateString('en-US',{month:'short',day:'numeric'});
const full=iso=>new Date(`${iso}T12:00:00`).toLocaleDateString('en-US',{month:'short',day:'numeric',year:'numeric'});
const period=p=>p?.start&&p?.end?`${full(p.start)} – ${full(p.end)}`:'Period unavailable';
const emptyTotals=()=>({clicks:null,impressions:null,ctr:null,position:null});
const emptyData=id=>({meta:{practice:practiceNames[id],domain:null,ga4Period:null,gscPeriod:null,rankingPrevious:null,rankingCurrent:null},ga4:{totalUsers:null,newUsers:null,sessions:null,engagedSessions:null,organicUsers:null,organicSessions:null,organicNewLeads:null,allChannelLeadEvents:null,channels:[],landingPages:[]},searchConsole:{daily:[],summary:null,queries:[],pages:[]},keywordRanking:[],organicLeads:{leadReportSourceRows:{}}});
function aggregateGA(items){
  const gs=items.map(([,d])=>d.ga4),aggregate=(k)=>gs.every(g=>g[k]!=null)?sum(gs,k):null;
  const channels=new Map();
  for(const g of gs)for(const c of g.channels){const v=channels.get(c.name)||{name:c.name,sessions:0,engagedSessions:0,secondsWeighted:0};v.sessions+=c.sessions;v.engagedSessions+=c.engagedSessions;v.secondsWeighted+=c.avgEngagementSeconds*c.sessions;channels.set(c.name,v)}
  return {totalUsers:aggregate('totalUsers'),newUsers:aggregate('newUsers'),sessions:aggregate('sessions'),engagedSessions:aggregate('engagedSessions'),organicUsers:aggregate('organicUsers'),organicSessions:aggregate('organicSessions'),organicNewLeads:aggregate('organicNewLeads'),allChannelLeadEvents:aggregate('allChannelLeadEvents'),channels:[...channels.values()].map(v=>({name:v.name,sessions:v.sessions,engagedSessions:v.engagedSessions,avgEngagementSeconds:v.secondsWeighted/v.sessions})),landingPages:items.flatMap(([id,d])=>d.ga4.landingPages.map(r=>({...r,practice:id,domain:d.meta.domain||'https://www.jamesrivercardiology.com'})))};
}
function allData(){
  const items=Object.entries(practiceData).filter(([,d])=>d);
  const alignedGA=items.every(([,d])=>d.meta.ga4Period?.start===items[0][1].meta.ga4Period?.start&&d.meta.ga4Period?.end===items[0][1].meta.ga4Period?.end);
  const sourceRows={};for(const [id,d] of items)for(const [source,count] of Object.entries(d.organicLeads.leadReportSourceRows||{}))sourceRows[`${practiceNames[id]} · ${source}`]=count;
  return {meta:{practice:'All Practices',domain:null,ga4Period:alignedGA?items[0][1].meta.ga4Period:null,gscPeriod:null,rankingPrevious:null,rankingCurrent:null,coverage:`${items.length} of ${Object.keys(practiceData).length} practices with supplied data`},ga4:alignedGA?aggregateGA(items):emptyData('all').ga4,searchConsole:{daily:[],summary:null,queries:items.flatMap(([id,d])=>d.searchConsole.queries.map(r=>({...r,practice:id}))),pages:items.flatMap(([id,d])=>d.searchConsole.pages.map(r=>({...r,practice:id,domain:d.meta.domain||'https://www.jamesrivercardiology.com'})))},keywordRanking:items.flatMap(([id,d])=>d.keywordRanking.map(r=>({...r,practice:id}))),organicLeads:{leadReportSourceRows:sourceRows}};
}
function selectedData(){return state.practice==='all'?allData():practiceData[state.practice]||emptyData(state.practice)}
let dashboardData=selectedData();
function gscAggregate(rows){if(!rows.length)return emptyTotals();const clicks=sum(rows,'clicks'),impressions=sum(rows,'impressions');return {clicks,impressions,ctr:rate(clicks,impressions),position:rate(rows.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.position*r.impressions,0),impressions)}}
function gscParts(){const daily=dashboardData.searchConsole.daily;if(daily.length>=28)return {previous:daily.slice(-28,-14),current:daily.slice(-14)};return {previous:[],current:daily}}
function selectedGSC(){const p=gscParts();return state.view==='previous'?p.previous:p.current}
function fullGSC(){const s=dashboardData.searchConsole;return s.summary?{...s.summary,ctr:rate(s.summary.clicks,s.summary.impressions)}:gscAggregate(s.daily)}
function selectedGSCMetrics(){if(state.view==='previous')return gscAggregate(gscParts().previous);return dashboardData.searchConsole.summary?fullGSC():gscAggregate(gscParts().current)}
function previousGSCMetrics(){return gscAggregate(gscParts().previous)}
function gaAvailable(){return state.view==='current'&&dashboardData.ga4.sessions!=null}
function movement(now,prior,lower=false){if(now==null||prior==null||prior===0)return null;const change=(now-prior)/prior;return {change,good:lower?change<0:change>0}}
function movementHTML(now,prior,lower=false){const m=movement(now,prior,lower);if(!m)return '<span class="muted">Previous: N/A</span>';return `<span class="delta ${Math.abs(m.change)<.0005?'neutral':m.good?'good':'bad'}">${m.change>0?'↑':m.change<0?'↓':'→'} ${Math.abs(m.change*100).toFixed(1)}%</span> <span class="muted">vs previous</span>`}
function mini(id,rows){$(id).innerHTML=rows.map(([label,value,note])=>`<article class="mini"><div class="metric-label">${label}</div><strong>${value}</strong><small>${esc(note||'Source export')}</small></article>`).join('')}
const definitions=[['Reported Lead Rows','All rows in the supplied practice lead workbook, across its full history and all self-reported sources; not a verified organic total.'],['Total Users','GA4 users summed across first-user channel groups.'],['Organic Users','GA4 users whose first-user channel is Organic Search.'],['Organic Sessions','GA4 sessions whose session channel is Organic Search.'],['Engagement Rate','Engaged sessions divided by sessions, all channels.'],['Average Engagement Time','Session-weighted average engagement time.'],['GA4 Organic New Leads','GA4 new leads attributed to first-user Organic Search in the selected GA4 week.'],['Organic Lead Conversion Rate','Organic new leads divided by first-user Organic Search users when available.'],['Google Search Clicks','Clicks in this practice’s selected Search Console period.'],['Google Search Impressions','Impressions in this practice’s selected Search Console period.'],['Average CTR','Search Console clicks divided by impressions.'],['Average Search Position','Impression-weighted position when daily data exists, otherwise weighted device summary.']];
function metricCard(label,value,formatted,prior,lower,source){const tip=definitions.find(x=>x[0]===label)?.[1]||label;return `<article class="kpi"><div class="metric-label">${label} <span class="metric-help" title="${esc(tip)}" aria-label="${esc(tip)}" tabindex="0">ⓘ</span></div><div class="metric-main"><strong class="metric-value">${formatted}</strong></div><div class="comparison">${movementHTML(value,prior,lower)}</div><small class="metric-source">${esc(source)}</small></article>`}
function renderKpis(){
  const ga=dashboardData.ga4,g=selectedGSCMetrics(),p=previousGSCMetrics(),available=gaAvailable(),eng=rate(ga.engagedSessions,ga.sessions),leadRate=rate(ga.organicNewLeads,ga.organicUsers),avg=rate(ga.channels.reduce((n,c)=>n+c.avgEngagementSeconds*c.sessions,0),ga.sessions),gaSource=dashboardData.meta.ga4Period?`GA4 · ${period(dashboardData.meta.ga4Period)}`:'GA4 · Not supplied',gscSource=dashboardData.meta.gscPeriod?`GSC · ${selectedGSC().length?`${full(selectedGSC()[0].date)} – ${full(selectedGSC().at(-1).date)}`:period(dashboardData.meta.gscPeriod)}`:'GSC · No aligned aggregate';
  const reportRows=Object.values(dashboardData.organicLeads.leadReportSourceRows||{}).reduce((n,x)=>n+x,0),hasReport=Object.keys(dashboardData.organicLeads.leadReportSourceRows||{}).length>0,reportSource='Lead workbook · full supplied history, date columns unlabeled · all self-reported sources';
  const cards=[['Total Users',available?ga.totalUsers:null,fmt(available?ga.totalUsers:null),null,false,gaSource],['Organic Users',available?ga.organicUsers:null,fmt(available?ga.organicUsers:null),null,false,gaSource],['Organic Sessions',available?ga.organicSessions:null,fmt(available?ga.organicSessions:null),null,false,gaSource],['Engagement Rate',available?eng:null,percent(available?eng:null),null,false,gaSource],['Average Engagement Time',available?avg:null,duration(available?avg:null),null,false,gaSource],['GA4 Organic New Leads',available?ga.organicNewLeads:null,fmt(available?ga.organicNewLeads:null),null,false,gaSource],['Reported Lead Rows',hasReport?reportRows:null,fmt(hasReport?reportRows:null),null,false,reportSource],['Organic Lead Conversion Rate',available?leadRate:null,percent(available?leadRate:null),null,false,gaSource],['Google Search Clicks',g.clicks,fmt(g.clicks),state.view==='current'?p.clicks:null,false,gscSource],['Google Search Impressions',g.impressions,fmt(g.impressions),state.view==='current'?p.impressions:null,false,gscSource],['Average CTR',g.ctr,percent(g.ctr),state.view==='current'?p.ctr:null,false,gscSource],['Average Search Position',g.position,pos(g.position),state.view==='current'?p.position:null,true,gscSource]];
  $('executiveKpis').innerHTML=cards.map(x=>metricCard(...x)).join('');
  mini('gaMetrics',[[ga.totalUsers==null&&ga.activeUsers!=null?'Active Users':'Users',fmt(available?(ga.totalUsers??ga.activeUsers):null),ga.totalUsers==null?'GA4 active users; total users not supplied':'First-user channel report'],['New Users',fmt(available?ga.newUsers:null),'First-user channel report'],['Sessions',fmt(available?ga.sessions:null),'Session channel report'],['Organic Sessions',fmt(available?ga.organicSessions:null),'Organic Search session channel'],['Engagement Rate',percent(available?eng:null),'All-channel sessions'],['Avg. Engagement Time',duration(available?avg:null),'Weighted by sessions'],['Lead Events',fmt(available?ga.allChannelLeadEvents:null),'generate_lead event, if supplied'],['Organic New Leads',fmt(available?ga.organicNewLeads:null),'First-user Organic Search']]);
  const all=fullGSC();mini('gscMetrics',[['Clicks',fmt(g.clicks),'Selected export period'],['Impressions',fmt(g.impressions),'Selected export period'],['CTR',percent(g.ctr),'Clicks / impressions'],['Avg. Position',pos(g.position),'Available source summary'],['Full Export Clicks',fmt(all.clicks),dashboardData.meta.gscPeriod?period(dashboardData.meta.gscPeriod):'Not comparable across practices'],['Full Export Impressions',fmt(all.impressions),dashboardData.meta.gscPeriod?period(dashboardData.meta.gscPeriod):'Not comparable across practices']]);
  const sources=dashboardData.organicLeads.leadReportSourceRows||{},sourceCount=(name)=>Object.keys(sources).filter(k=>k.endsWith(name)).reduce((n,k)=>n+sources[k],0);mini('leadMetrics',[['Reported Lead Rows',fmt(hasReport?reportRows:null),'Full workbook · all sources, dates unlabeled'],['Self-Reported Online Search',fmt(hasReport?sourceCount('Self - Online Search'):null),'May include paid search'],['Self-Reported Practice Website',fmt(hasReport?sourceCount('Self - Practice Website'):null),'Organic attribution not verified'],['GA4 Organic New Leads',fmt(available?ga.organicNewLeads:null),'Selected GA4 week · first-user Organic Search'],['GA4 Organic Lead Rate',percent(available?leadRate:null),'GA4 organic new leads / organic users']]);
}
function rankStatus(r){if(r.currentRank==null)return r.previousRank!=null&&r.reportedMovement==='Lost'?'Reported Lost':'Unranked';if(r.previousRank!=null)return r.currentRank<r.previousRank?'Improved':r.currentRank>r.previousRank?'Declined':'No Change';return r.reportedMovement==='Lost'?'Reported Lost':'No Prior Rank'}
function rankChange(r){return r.currentRank==null||r.previousRank==null?null:r.previousRank-r.currentRank}
function badge(label){const cls={'Improved':'improved','Declined':'declined','No Change':'stable','No Prior Rank':'new','Reported Lost':'attention','Unranked':'attention'}[label]||'stable';return `<span class="badge ${cls}">${esc(label)}</span>`}
function rankSummary(){const rows=dashboardData.keywordRanking;return {total:rows.length,top3:rows.filter(r=>r.currentRank!=null&&r.currentRank<=3).length,top10:rows.filter(r=>r.currentRank!=null&&r.currentRank<=10).length,top20:rows.filter(r=>r.currentRank!=null&&r.currentRank<=20).length,top50:rows.filter(r=>r.currentRank!=null&&r.currentRank<=50).length,improved:rows.filter(r=>rankStatus(r)==='Improved').length,declined:rows.filter(r=>rankStatus(r)==='Declined').length,reportedLost:rows.filter(r=>rankStatus(r)==='Reported Lost').length}}
function filteredRankings(){const rows=dashboardData.keywordRanking.filter(r=>(state.status==='all'||rankStatus(r)===state.status)&&`${r.keyword} ${r.location} ${r.url||''} ${practiceNames[r.practice||state.practice]||''}`.toLowerCase().includes(state.keywordSearch));rows.sort((a,b)=>state.rankSort==='change'?(rankChange(b)??-999)-(rankChange(a)??-999):state.rankSort==='keyword'?a.keyword.localeCompare(b.keyword):state.rankSort==='location'?a.location.localeCompare(b.location):(a.currentRank??Infinity)-(b.currentRank??Infinity));return rows}
function renderRankings(){const k=rankSummary();mini('rankMetrics',[['Tracked Rows',k.total,'Keyword × location'],['Top 3',k.top3,'Current rank'],['Top 10',k.top10,'Current rank'],['Top 20',k.top20,'Current rank'],['Top 50',k.top50,'Current rank'],['Improved',k.improved,'Numeric prior rank'],['Declined',k.declined,'Numeric prior rank'],['Reported Lost',k.reportedLost,'Previous rank present; no current rank']]);const rows=filteredRankings();$('keywordRows').innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(r=>{const change=rankChange(r),url=r.url?`<a href="${esc(r.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">${esc(new URL(r.url).pathname||'/')}</a>${r.urlSource==='previous'?' <small class="muted">(previous URL)</small>':''}`:NA;return `<tr><td>${esc(practiceNames[r.practice||state.practice])}</td><td>${esc(r.keyword)}</td><td>${esc(r.location)}</td><td>${fmt(r.currentRank)}</td><td>${fmt(r.previousRank)}</td><td>${change==null?NA:change>0?'↑ '+change:change<0?'↓ '+Math.abs(change):'—'}</td><td>${NA}</td><td>${NA}</td><td>${NA}</td><td>${url}</td><td>${badge(rankStatus(r))}</td></tr>`}).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="11" class="empty">No matching keyword data for this selection.</td></tr>'}
function renderQueries(){const rows=dashboardData.searchConsole.queries.filter(r=>r.keyword.toLowerCase().includes(state.querySearch)).sort((a,b)=>b.clicks-a.clicks||b.impressions-a.impressions),shown=rows.slice(0,100);$('queryRows').innerHTML=shown.length?shown.map(r=>`<tr><td>${esc(practiceNames[r.practice||state.practice])}</td><td>${esc(r.keyword)}</td><td>${fmt(r.clicks)}</td><td>${fmt(r.impressions)}</td><td>${percent(rate(r.clicks,r.impressions))}</td><td>${pos(r.position)}</td><td>${NA}</td></tr>`).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="7" class="empty">No matching query data for this selection.</td></tr>';$('queryCount').textContent=`Showing ${shown.length} of ${rows.length} query rows. Prior query ranks were not supplied.`}
function pageRows(){const map=new Map();const add=(id,domain,path,field,row)=>{const key=id+'|'+path;if(!map.has(key))map.set(key,{practice:id,domain,path,ga:null,gsc:null});const item=map.get(key);if(field==='ga'&&item.ga){item.ga={...item.ga,sessions:item.ga.sessions+row.sessions,activeUsers:null}}else if(field==='gsc'&&item.gsc){const impressions=item.gsc.impressions+row.impressions;item.gsc={...item.gsc,clicks:item.gsc.clicks+row.clicks,impressions,position:rate(item.gsc.position*item.gsc.impressions+row.position*row.impressions,impressions)}}else item[field]=row};const items=state.practice==='all'?Object.entries(practiceData).filter(([,d])=>d):[[state.practice,dashboardData]];for(const [id,d] of items){const domain=d.meta.domain||'https://www.jamesrivercardiology.com';d.ga4.landingPages.forEach(r=>add(id,domain,r.path,'ga',r));d.searchConsole.pages.forEach(r=>add(id,domain,r.path,'gsc',r))}return [...map.values()]}
function sortedPages(){const rows=pageRows();rows.sort((a,b)=>{const val=r=>state.pageSort==='users'?r.ga?.activeUsers:state.pageSort==='sessions'?r.ga?.sessions:state.pageSort==='ctr'?rate(r.gsc?.clicks,r.gsc?.impressions):r.gsc?.[state.pageSort];return (val(b)??-1)-(val(a)??-1)});return rows}
function renderPages(){$('pageRows').innerHTML=sortedPages().map(r=>`<tr><td>${esc(practiceNames[r.practice])}</td><td><a href="${esc(r.domain+r.path)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">${esc(r.path)}</a></td><td>${NA}</td><td>${fmt(r.ga?.activeUsers)}</td><td>${fmt(r.ga?.sessions)}</td><td>${NA}</td><td>${fmt(r.gsc?.clicks)}</td><td>${fmt(r.gsc?.impressions)}</td><td>${percent(rate(r.gsc?.clicks,r.gsc?.impressions))}</td><td>${pos(r.gsc?.position)}</td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="10" class="empty">No page data for this practice.</td></tr>'}
function buildMonthly(data=dashboardData){const rows=[],groups=new Map();for(const r of data.searchConsole.daily){const key=r.date.slice(0,7);if(!groups.has(key))groups.set(key,[]);groups.get(key).push(r)}for(const [key,part] of groups){const g=gscAggregate(part);rows.push({key:key+'-01',label:`${new Date(`${key}-01T12:00:00`).toLocaleDateString('en-US',{month:'long',year:'numeric'})} (${short(part[0].date)}–${short(part.at(-1).date)} GSC)`,clicks:g.clicks,impressions:g.impressions,ctr:g.ctr,position:g.position})}if(!groups.size&&data.searchConsole.summary&&data.meta.gscPeriod){const g={...data.searchConsole.summary,ctr:rate(data.searchConsole.summary.clicks,data.searchConsole.summary.impressions)},p=data.meta.gscPeriod;rows.push({key:p.start,label:`${short(p.start)}–${short(p.end)} GSC (partial month)`,clicks:g.clicks,impressions:g.impressions,ctr:g.ctr,position:g.position})}if(data.ga4.sessions!=null&&data.meta.ga4Period){const p=data.meta.ga4Period;rows.push({key:p.end,label:`${short(p.start)}–${short(p.end)} GA4 (weekly)`,organicUsers:data.ga4.organicUsers,organicSessions:data.ga4.organicSessions,organicLeads:data.ga4.organicNewLeads,conversion:rate(data.ga4.organicNewLeads,data.ga4.organicUsers)})}return rows}
function renderMonthly(){let rows;if(state.practice==='all'){rows=Object.entries(practiceData).filter(([,d])=>d).flatMap(([id,d])=>{return buildMonthly(d).map(x=>({...x,label:`${practiceNames[id]} · ${x.label}`}))})}else rows=buildMonthly();rows.sort((a,b)=>state.monthlySort==='dateAsc'?a.key.localeCompare(b.key):state.monthlySort==='clicks'?(b.clicks??-1)-(a.clicks??-1):b.key.localeCompare(a.key));$('monthlyRows').innerHTML=rows.map(r=>`<tr><td>${esc(r.label)}</td><td>${fmt(r.organicUsers)}</td><td>${fmt(r.organicSessions)}</td><td>${fmt(r.clicks)}</td><td>${fmt(r.impressions)}</td><td>${percent(r.ctr)}</td><td>${pos(r.position)}</td><td>${fmt(r.organicLeads)}</td><td>${percent(r.conversion)}</td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="9" class="empty">No monthly or weekly export for this practice.</td></tr>'}
function renderLeads(){const ga=dashboardData.ga4,p=dashboardData.meta.ga4Period,available=gaAvailable()&&ga.organicNewLeads!=null;$('dailyLeadRows').innerHTML=available?`<tr><td>${short(p.start)}–${short(p.end)}, 2026</td><td>${NA}</td><td>GA4 new leads</td><td>First-user Organic Search</td><td>${fmt(ga.organicNewLeads)}</td><td>${percent(rate(ga.organicNewLeads,ga.organicUsers))} <span class="muted">of organic users</span></td></tr>`:`<tr><td colspan="6" class="empty">Verified organic lead count not available for this selection.</td></tr>`}
function renderInsights(){const ga=dashboardData.ga4,k=rankSummary(),g=selectedGSCMetrics(),p=previousGSCMetrics(),lines=[];if(g.clicks!=null&&p.clicks!=null){const d=movement(g.clicks,p.clicks),im=movement(g.impressions,p.impressions);lines.push(['SEARCH CLICKS',`GSC clicks ${d.change>=0?'rose':'fell'} ${Math.abs(d.change*100).toFixed(1)}% between the two comparable 14-day windows.`],['SEARCH VISIBILITY',`GSC impressions ${im.change>=0?'rose':'fell'} ${Math.abs(im.change*100).toFixed(1)}% across those windows.`])}else if(g.clicks!=null)lines.push(['SEARCH',`${fmt(g.clicks)} clicks and ${fmt(g.impressions)} impressions in ${period(dashboardData.meta.gscPeriod)}; no earlier comparable period supplied.`]);if(k.total)lines.push(['RANKINGS',`${k.total} tracked rows; ${k.improved} improved, ${k.declined} declined, and ${k.reportedLost} are reported lost.`]);const leadRows=Object.values(dashboardData.organicLeads.leadReportSourceRows||{}).reduce((n,x)=>n+x,0);if(leadRows)lines.push(['LEAD REPORT',`${fmt(leadRows)} rows across all self-reported sources in the supplied lead workbook; its date columns are unlabeled and these rows are not a verified organic total.`]);if(ga.organicNewLeads!=null)lines.push(['LEADS',`${fmt(ga.organicNewLeads)} GA4 new leads attributed to first-user Organic Search in ${period(dashboardData.meta.ga4Period)}.`]);else if(ga.sessions!=null)lines.push(['LEADS','Organic lead attribution was not included in this practice’s supplied GA4 export.']);const pages=dashboardData.ga4.landingPages;if(pages.length){const top=[...pages].sort((a,b)=>b.sessions-a.sessions)[0];lines.push(['LANDING PAGE',`${top.path} recorded ${fmt(top.sessions)} GA4 landing sessions from all channels.`])}if(state.practice==='all')lines.unshift(['COVERAGE',`All Practices includes ${dashboardData.meta.coverage}. GSC periods differ, so combined GSC totals are unavailable.`]);$('managementInsights').innerHTML=lines.map(([label,message])=>`<article class="insight-card"><span>${label}</span><p>${esc(message)}</p></article>`).join('')||'<article class="insight-card"><p>Data not available for this practice.</p></article>';
  const actions=[],op=dashboardData.keywordRanking.filter(r=>r.currentRank>=11&&r.currentRank<=20),all=fullGSC(),low=dashboardData.searchConsole.queries.filter(r=>all.ctr!=null&&r.impressions>=Math.max(100,all.impressions*.005)&&rate(r.clicks,r.impressions)<all.ctr),declines=dashboardData.keywordRanking.filter(r=>rankStatus(r)==='Declined');if(op.length)actions.push(['High','Review keywords ranking 11–20',`${op.length} tracked rows currently rank 11–20.`,'Keyword SEO']);if(low.length)actions.push(['High','Review high-impression, lower-CTR queries',`${low.length} query rows fall below this export’s ${percent(all.ctr)} CTR.`,'Search snippets']);if(declines.length)actions.push(['Medium','Inspect declining tracked keywords',`${declines.length} rows have a lower current rank than previous.`,'On-page SEO']);if(dashboardData.keywordRanking.some(r=>rankStatus(r)==='Reported Lost'))actions.push(['High','Review lost tracked rankings',`${k.reportedLost} rows have a reported lost status or no current position after a previous rank.`,'Keyword SEO']);if(ga.sessions!=null&&ga.organicNewLeads==null)actions.push(['High','Confirm organic lead attribution','Organic users are reported but attributed new leads are not supplied.','Measurement']);$('actionRows').innerHTML=actions.map(([priority,action,evidence,area])=>`<tr><td>${priority}</td><td>${esc(action)}</td><td>${esc(evidence)}</td><td>${area}</td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="4" class="empty">No source-backed action items available.</td></tr>'}
function clearChart(id){if(state.charts[id]){state.charts[id].destroy();delete state.charts[id]}}
function unavailable(boxId,id,message){clearChart(id);const box=$(boxId);box.querySelector('canvas').hidden=true;let p=box.querySelector('.unavailable');if(!p){p=document.createElement('p');p.className='unavailable';box.append(p)}p.textContent=message}
function showCanvas(boxId){const target=$(boxId),box=target.tagName==='CANVAS'?target.parentElement:target;box.querySelector('canvas').hidden=false;const p=box.querySelector('.unavailable');if(p)p.remove()}
function chart(id,type,labels,datasets,options={}){clearChart(id);if(!window.Chart)return;const dark=document.body.classList.contains('dark'),ink=dark?'#a2b6c5':'#64778a',grid=dark?'#2d4353':'#e0e9ef',base={responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,interaction:{mode:'index',intersect:false},plugins:{legend:{display:datasets.length>1||type==='doughnut',position:'bottom',labels:{color:ink}},tooltip:{enabled:true}},indexAxis:options.horizontal?'y':'x'};if(type!=='doughnut')base.scales={x:{grid:{display:false},ticks:{color:ink,maxTicksLimit:9}},y:{beginAtZero:!options.reverse,reverse:!!options.reverse,grid:{color:grid},ticks:{color:ink}},...(options.secondAxis?{y1:{position:'right',grid:{display:false},ticks:{color:ink}}}:{})};if(options.percent&&base.scales)(options.horizontal?base.scales.x:base.scales.y).ticks.callback=v=>`${v}%`;if(type==='doughnut')base.cutout='65%';state.charts[id]=new Chart($(id),{type,data:{labels,datasets},options:base})}
const line=(label,data,color,axis)=>({label,data,borderColor:color,backgroundColor:color,borderWidth:2,tension:.25,pointRadius:data.length>18?0:2,pointHoverRadius:5,yAxisID:axis||'y'});
function renderCharts(){if(!window.Chart){document.querySelectorAll('.chart-box').forEach(b=>{if(!b.querySelector('.unavailable')){const p=document.createElement('p');p.className='unavailable';p.textContent='Charts unavailable; tables remain accessible.';b.append(p)}});return}const ga=dashboardData.ga4,teal='#087e82',blue='#3266a8',gold='#d4a250',rows=selectedGSC(),labels=rows.map(r=>short(r.date));unavailable('trafficBox','trafficChart','Daily organic GA4 traffic was not included in the supplied exports.');const channels=gaAvailable()?ga.channels.filter(c=>c.sessions>0):[];if(channels.length){for(const id of ['sourceChart','landingChart','engagementChart'])showCanvas(id);chart('sourceChart','doughnut',channels.map(c=>c.name),[{label:'Sessions',data:channels.map(c=>c.sessions),backgroundColor:[teal,blue,'#79abb9',gold,'#b6cbd4','#b9d8ca','#8ca1b1'],borderWidth:0}]);chart('landingChart','bar',['GA4 · '+(dashboardData.meta.practice||'selected practices')],[{label:ga.totalUsers==null?'Active users':'Total users',data:[ga.totalUsers??ga.activeUsers],backgroundColor:teal,borderRadius:5},{label:'Sessions',data:[ga.sessions],backgroundColor:blue,borderRadius:5}]);chart('engagementChart','bar',channels.map(c=>c.name),[{label:'Engagement rate',data:channels.map(c=>rate(c.engagedSessions,c.sessions)*100),backgroundColor:teal,borderRadius:5}],{horizontal:true,percent:true})}else for(const [box,id] of [['sourceChart','sourceChart'],['landingChart','landingChart'],['engagementChart','engagementChart']])unavailable(id,box,'GA4 data not available for this practice.');if(gaAvailable()&&ga.organicNewLeads!=null){showCanvas('gaLeadsBox');chart('gaLeadsChart','bar',[short(dashboardData.meta.ga4Period.start)+'–'+short(dashboardData.meta.ga4Period.end)],[{label:'Organic new leads',data:[ga.organicNewLeads],backgroundColor:teal,borderRadius:5}])}else unavailable('gaLeadsBox','gaLeadsChart','Verified organic new lead count not supplied.');if(rows.length){for(const [box] of [['searchBox'],['positionBox'],['ctrBox']])showCanvas(box);chart('searchChart','line',labels,[line('Clicks',rows.map(r=>r.clicks),teal),line('Impressions',rows.map(r=>r.impressions),blue,'y1')],{secondAxis:true});chart('positionChart','line',labels,[line('Average position',rows.map(r=>r.position),blue)],{reverse:true});chart('ctrChart','line',labels,[line('CTR',rows.map(r=>rate(r.clicks,r.impressions)*100),teal)],{percent:true})}else{const message=dashboardData.searchConsole.summary?'Daily GSC rows were not supplied; summary KPIs and query/page tables remain available.':'No comparable daily GSC data for this selection.';for(const [box,id] of [['searchBox','searchChart'],['positionBox','positionChart'],['ctrBox','ctrChart']])unavailable(box,id,message)}const ranks=dashboardData.keywordRanking,buckets=[ranks.filter(r=>r.currentRank!=null&&r.currentRank<=3).length,ranks.filter(r=>r.currentRank>=4&&r.currentRank<=10).length,ranks.filter(r=>r.currentRank>=11&&r.currentRank<=20).length,ranks.filter(r=>r.currentRank>=21&&r.currentRank<=50).length,ranks.filter(r=>r.currentRank>50).length,ranks.filter(r=>r.currentRank==null).length];showCanvas('distributionChart');chart('distributionChart','bar',['Top 3','4–10','11–20','21–50','51+','Unranked'],[{label:'Tracked rows',data:buckets,backgroundColor:[teal,'#2b9b9d',blue,'#86a8c7','#bfd1dc',gold],borderRadius:5}]);if(gaAvailable()&&ga.organicNewLeads!=null){showCanvas('leadsBox');chart('leadsChart','bar',[short(dashboardData.meta.ga4Period.start)+'–'+short(dashboardData.meta.ga4Period.end)],[{label:'GA4 organic new leads',data:[ga.organicNewLeads],backgroundColor:teal,borderRadius:5}])}else unavailable('leadsBox','leadsChart','Verified organic lead count not supplied.');const sources=Object.entries(dashboardData.organicLeads.leadReportSourceRows);if(sources.length){showCanvas('sourceLeadsChart');chart('sourceLeadsChart','bar',sources.map(([name])=>name.replace('Self - ','')),[{label:'Source report rows, not organic attribution',data:sources.map(([,n])=>n),backgroundColor:blue,borderRadius:5}],{horizontal:true})}else unavailable('sourceLeadsChart','sourceLeadsChart','Lead source report not supplied.');const top=[...ga.landingPages].sort((a,b)=>b.sessions-a.sessions).slice(0,5);if(top.length){showCanvas('pageChart');chart('pageChart','bar',top.map(p=>p.path),[{label:'GA4 sessions · all channels',data:top.map(p=>p.sessions),backgroundColor:teal,borderRadius:5}],{horizontal:true})}else unavailable('pageChart','pageChart','Landing page data not available.') }
function render(){dashboardData=selectedData();const ga=dashboardData.ga4,meta=dashboardData.meta,rows=selectedGSC(),g=selectedGSCMetrics(),p=previousGSCMetrics(),hasPrevious=p.clicks!=null;const coverage=state.practice==='all'?` · ${meta.coverage}`:'';$('currentDate').textContent=`Viewing: ${meta.practice}${coverage}`;$('sourceCoverage').textContent=`${Object.values(practiceData).filter(Boolean).length} of ${Object.keys(practiceData).length} practices available`;$('lastUpdated').textContent='Source exports · Sep 28, 2026';$('weekLabel').textContent=state.view==='current'?'Current Period':state.view==='previous'?'Previous Period':'Monthly View';$('overviewTitle').textContent=`${meta.practice} · ${state.view==='previous'?'Previous period':state.view==='monthly'?'Monthly coverage':'Current performance'}`;$('periodLabel').textContent=state.view==='monthly'?'Partial month and weekly exports are labeled below':`GA4: ${period(meta.ga4Period)} · GSC: ${period(meta.gscPeriod)}`;$('comparisonLabel').textContent=hasPrevious?'GSC: latest 14 days compared with prior 14 days':state.practice==='all'?'GSC reporting windows differ; no combined total':'Previous-period comparison unavailable';$('gaPeriod').textContent=meta.ga4Period?`${period(meta.ga4Period)} · GA4${meta.sourceProperty?` property: ${meta.sourceProperty}`:''}`:'GA4 data unavailable';$('gscPeriod').textContent=meta.gscPeriod?`${period(meta.gscPeriod)} · Web search`:'GSC periods not aligned';renderKpis();renderRankings();renderQueries();renderPages();renderMonthly();renderLeads();renderInsights();renderCharts();let note;if(state.practice==='all')note=`All Practices combines available GA4 session counts for ${period(meta.ga4Period)}. Total users, combined GSC totals, and verified organic leads are N/A where source coverage or attribution differs.`;else if(hasPrevious&&state.view==='current'){const m=movement(g.clicks,p.clicks);note=`GSC clicks ${m.change>=0?'increased':'decreased'} ${Math.abs(m.change*100).toFixed(1)}% in the latest 14 days versus the previous 14 days.`}else if(g.clicks!=null)note=`Search Console recorded ${fmt(g.clicks)} clicks and ${fmt(g.impressions)} impressions in ${period(meta.gscPeriod)}.`;else note='No source data is available for this practice and period.';const leadRows=Object.values(dashboardData.organicLeads.leadReportSourceRows||{}).reduce((n,x)=>n+x,0);$('quickInsight').innerHTML=`<strong>${esc(meta.practice)}</strong> · ${esc(state.practice==='james'?`${fmt(leadRows)} rows in the full JRC lead report across all self-reported sources; the GA4 organic new-lead count of ${fmt(ga.organicNewLeads)} covers ${period(meta.ga4Period)}. `:'')}${esc(note)}`}
function csvExport(){const rows=filteredRankings(),headers=['Practice','Keyword','Location','Previous Rank','Current Rank','Change','Reported Movement','Search Volume','Category','Priority','URL','SEO Status'],quote=v=>`"${String(v??'').replace(/"/g,'""')}"`,csv=[[`${dashboardData.meta.practice} keyword rankings`],[],headers,...rows.map(r=>[practiceNames[r.practice||state.practice],r.keyword,r.location,r.previousRank,r.currentRank,rankChange(r),r.reportedMovement,r.searchVolume,r.category,r.priority,r.url,rankStatus(r)])].map(row=>row.map(quote).join(',')).join('\r\n'),url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob(['\ufeff'+csv],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8'})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=`alignedcardio-${state.practice}-keyword-rankings-2026-09-27.csv`;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);$('exportMenu').hidden=true}
let toastTimer;function toast(message){$('toast').textContent=message;$('toast').classList.add('show');clearTimeout(toastTimer);toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>$('toast').classList.remove('show'),4500)}
$('practiceSelect').addEventListener('change',e=>{state.practice=e.target.value;state.keywordSearch='';state.querySearch='';state.status='all';$('keywordSearch').value='';$('querySearch').value='';$('statusFilter').value='all';render()});
$('rangeSelect').addEventListener('change',e=>{state.view=e.target.value;render()});
$('keywordSearch').addEventListener('input',e=>{state.keywordSearch=e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase();renderRankings()});
$('querySearch').addEventListener('input',e=>{state.querySearch=e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase();renderQueries()});
$('statusFilter').addEventListener('change',e=>{state.status=e.target.value;renderRankings()});
$('rankSort').addEventListener('change',e=>{state.rankSort=e.target.value;renderRankings()});
$('pageSort').addEventListener('change',e=>{state.pageSort=e.target.value;renderPages()});
$('monthlySort').addEventListener('change',e=>{state.monthlySort=e.target.value;renderMonthly()});
try{if(localStorage.getItem('alignedcardio-theme')==='dark')document.body.classList.add('dark')}catch(_){}
$('themeButton').addEventListener('click',()=>{document.body.classList.toggle('dark');const dark=document.body.classList.contains('dark');try{localStorage.setItem('alignedcardio-theme',dark?'dark':'light')}catch(_){}$('themeButton').setAttribute('aria-label',`Switch to ${dark?'light':'dark'} mode`);renderCharts()});
function closeMenu(){$('sidebar').classList.remove('open');$('scrim').hidden=true;$('menuButton').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false')}
$('menuButton').addEventListener('click',()=>{const open=$('sidebar').classList.toggle('open');$('scrim').hidden=!open;$('menuButton').setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(open))});$('scrim').addEventListener('click',closeMenu);
document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar nav a').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener('click',()=>{document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar nav a').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('active'));a.classList.add('active');closeMenu()}));
$('printHeader').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('exportMenu').hidden=!$('exportMenu').hidden});$('exportCsv').addEventListener('click',csvExport);$('printReport').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('exportMenu').hidden=true;window.print()});$('downloadPdf').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('exportMenu').hidden=true;toast('Choose “Save as PDF” in the print dialog.');setTimeout(()=>window.print(),350)});document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(!$('exportMenu').contains(e.target)&&e.target!==$('printHeader'))$('exportMenu').hidden=true});render();

  </script>
</body>
</html>
